                    <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:"0.84rem",color:T.text1}}>Phase {i+1}: {ph.phase}</div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:"0.68rem",color:T.text2}}>{e?.name||ph.owner} · {ph.duration}</div>
                  </div>
                  {pt.length>0&&<div style={{textAlign:"right",minWidth:64}}><div style={{fontSize:"0.68rem",color:T.text3,marginBottom:3}}>{done}/{pt.length}</div><ProgressBar value={pt.length?Math.round((done/pt.length)*100):0} color={e?.color||T.accent} height={3} /></div>}
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:5,marginBottom:7}}>{(ph.tasks||[]).map((t,j)=><span key={j} style={{background:T.surf,border:`1px solid ${T.border2}`,borderRadius:6,padding:"2px 8px",fontSize:"0.65rem",color:T.text2}}>{t}</span>)}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:"0.7rem",color:T.green}}>✓ {ph.deliverable}</div>
                {ph.dependencies&&ph.dependencies!=="none"&&<div style={{fontSize:"0.65rem",color:T.text3,marginTop:2}}>Needs: {ph.dependencies}</div>}
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {plan.successMetrics?.length>0&&<div style={{marginTop:12,padding:"12px 14px",background:`${T.green}08`,border:`1px solid ${T.green}22`,borderRadius:10}}><div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:"0.74rem",color:T.green,marginBottom:5}}>✅ Success Metrics</div>{plan.successMetrics.map((m,i)=><div key={i} style={{fontSize:"0.72rem",color:T.text2}}>• {m}</div>)}</div>}
          {plan.risks?.length>0&&<div style={{marginTop:8,padding:"12px 14px",background:`${T.red}08`,border:`1px solid ${T.red}22`,borderRadius:10}}><div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:"0.74rem",color:T.red,marginBottom:5}}>⚠️ Risks</div>{plan.risks.map((r,i)=><div key={i} style={{fontSize:"0.72rem",color:T.text2}}>• {r}</div>)}</div>}
        </div>
      ) : !autoRunning && (
        <div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"60px 20px",color:T.text3}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:40,marginBottom:12}}>🗂️</div>
          <div style={{fontSize:"0.84rem"}}>No active plan. Enter a goal above and click Generate Plan.</div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

// ── REVIEWS VIEW ─────────────────────────────────────────
const ReviewsView = ({ S, T, EMPS }) => {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("all");
  const filtered = (S.reviews||[]).filter(r=>tab==="all"?true:tab==="approved"?r.approved:!r.approved);
  const avg = S.reviews?.length ? Math.round((S.reviews||[]).reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.score||0),0)/S.reviews.length) : 0;
  return (
    <div style={{padding:18}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,marginBottom:14,flexWrap:"wrap",alignItems:"center"}}>
        {[["all","All"],["approved","Approved"],["rejected","Needs Work"]].map(([f,l])=>(
          <button key={f} onClick={()=>setTab(f)} style={{padding:"5px 14px",background:tab===f?T.accent:T.surf,border:`1px solid ${tab===f?T.accent:T.border2}`,borderRadius:20,color:tab===f?"#fff":T.text2,fontSize:"0.74rem",fontWeight:tab===f?700:400,cursor:"pointer"}}>
            {l} ({f==="all"?(S.reviews||[]).length:f==="approved"?(S.reviews||[]).filter(r=>r.approved).length:(S.reviews||[]).filter(r=>!r.approved).length})
          </button>
        ))}
        <div style={{marginLeft:"auto",fontSize:"0.74rem",color:T.text2}}>Avg Score: <strong style={{color:T.green}}>{avg}</strong>/100</div>
      </div>
      {filtered.length===0?(
        <div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"60px 0",color:T.text3}}><div style={{fontSize:40,marginBottom:12}}>🔍</div><div>No reviews yet. Run Autonomous Mode to generate reviews automatically.</div></div>
      ):(
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(auto-fill,minmax(300px,1fr))",gap:12}}>
          {filtered.map((r,i)=>{
            const e=EMPS[r.empId]; const sc=r.score||0; const scC=sc>=80?T.green:sc>=60?T.yellow:T.red;
            return (
              <div key={r.id||i} style={{background:T.surf,border:`1px solid ${r.approved?T.green+"28":T.red+"28"}`,borderRadius:13,padding:16,borderTop:`3px solid ${r.approved?T.green:T.red}`}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,marginBottom:10}}>
                  <div style={{width:34,height:34,borderRadius:9,background:`${e?.color||T.accent}20`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:17}}>{e?.icon||"👤"}</div>
                  <div style={{flex:1}}><div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:"0.8rem",color:T.text1}}>{(r.taskTitle||"Task").slice(0,38)}</div><div style={{fontSize:"0.65rem",color:T.text2}}>{r.empName}</div></div>
                  <div style={{textAlign:"right"}}><div style={{fontSize:"1.4rem",fontWeight:900,color:scC,lineHeight:1}}>{sc}</div><div style={{fontSize:"0.58rem",color:T.text3}}>/100</div></div>
                </div>
                <ProgressBar value={sc} color={scC} height={4} />
                {r.feedback&&<div style={{marginTop:9,fontSize:"0.74rem",color:T.text2,lineHeight:1.5}}>{r.feedback}</div>}
                <div style={{marginTop:7,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:2}}>
                  {(r.strengths||[]).slice(0,2).map((s,j)=><div key={j} style={{fontSize:"0.68rem",color:T.green}}>✓ {s}</div>)}
                  {(r.gaps||[]).slice(0,2).map((g,j)=><div key={j} style={{fontSize:"0.68rem",color:T.yellow}}>⚠ {g}</div>)}
                </div>
                <div style={{marginTop:7,fontSize:"0.6rem",color:T.text3}}>{r.createdAt?new Date(r.createdAt).toLocaleString():""}</div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

// ── BUILD VIEW ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Multi-agent software pipeline: Research → Software Engineer → CTO → Reviewer
// Produces complete code files, downloadable as ZIP, exportable to GitHub.
const BuildView = ({ S, T, inp, buildSpec, setBuildSpec, buildName, setBuildName, buildRunning, buildStage, buildProgress, runBuild, stopBuild }) => {
  const builds = S.builds || [];
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState(builds[0]?.id || null);
  const [ghOpenId, setGhOpenId] = useState(null);
  const [ghToken, setGhToken] = useState("");
  const [ghOwner, setGhOwner] = useState("");
  const [ghRepo, setGhRepo] = useState("");
  const [ghBranch, setGhBranch] = useState("main");
  const [ghPushing, setGhPushing] = useState(false);
  const [ghMsg, setGhMsg] = useState(null);

  const statusColor = (s) => s==="completed"?T.green : s==="needs_revision"?T.yellow : s==="error"||s==="aborted"?T.red : T.cyan;
  const statusLabel = (s) => ({ completed:"✅ Completed", needs_revision:"⚠️ Needs Revision", error:"❌ Error", aborted:"⏹ Aborted", running:"⏳ Running" }[s] || s);

  const doGithubPush = async (build) => {
    setGhPushing(true); setGhMsg(null);
    try {
      await pushFilesToGithub({ token: ghToken, owner: ghOwner, repo: ghRepo, branch: ghBranch, files: build.files, commitMessage: `APEX OS build: ${build.name}` });
      setGhMsg({ type:"success", text:`Pushed ${build.files.length} file(s) to ${ghOwner}/${ghRepo}@${ghBranch}` });
    } catch(e) {
      setGhMsg({ type:"error", text: e.message });
    }
    setGhPushing(false);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
      <div style={{ background: T.surf, border: `1px solid ${T.accent}28`, borderRadius: 14, padding: 20, marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: "0.88rem", marginBottom: 6 }}>🛠️ AI Build Pipeline</div>
        <div style={{ color: T.text2, fontSize: "0.78rem", marginBottom: 12 }}>Describe what to build → Research Engineer briefs it → Software Engineer writes complete code → CTO reviews → Final QA → download ZIP or push to GitHub.</div>
        {buildRunning && <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}><ProgressBar value={buildProgress} color={T.accent} height={5} label={buildStage} /></div>}
        <input value={buildName} onChange={e=>setBuildName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Project name (optional)" disabled={buildRunning} style={{ ...inp, marginBottom: 9 }} />
        <div style={{ display:"flex", gap:9 }}>
          <input value={buildSpec} onChange={e=>setBuildSpec(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e=>e.key==="Enter"&&!buildRunning&&runBuild()} placeholder="e.g. A React todo app with due dates and local storage" disabled={buildRunning} style={{ ...inp, flex:1 }} />
          <button onClick={buildRunning ? stopBuild : ()=>runBuild()} style={{ padding:"10px 18px", background: buildRunning?`${T.red}18`:`linear-gradient(135deg,${T.accent},${T.accent2})`, border: buildRunning?`1px solid ${T.red}40`:"none", borderRadius:9, color: buildRunning?T.red:"#fff", fontWeight:700, fontSize:"0.8rem", cursor:"pointer", whiteSpace:"nowrap" }}>
            {buildRunning ? "⏹ Stop" : "🚀 Start Build"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {builds.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ textAlign:"center", padding:"60px 0", color:T.text3 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize:40, marginBottom:12 }}>🛠️</div>
          <div>No builds yet. Describe something to build above, or ask APEX in CEO Chat to build it for you.</div>
        </div>
      ) : builds.map(b => {
        const open = openId === b.id;
        return (
          <div key={b.id} style={{ background:T.surf, border:`1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius:14, padding:0, marginBottom:12, overflow:"hidden" }}>
            <div onClick={()=>setOpenId(open?null:b.id)} style={{ display:"flex", alignItems:"center", gap:10, padding:16, cursor:"pointer" }}>
              <div style={{ flex:1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontWeight:700, fontSize:"0.86rem", color:T.text1 }}>{b.name}</div>
                <div style={{ fontSize:"0.7rem", color:T.text3 }}>{new Date(b.createdAt).toLocaleString()} · {(b.files||[]).length} file(s)</div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ fontSize:"0.74rem", fontWeight:700, color:statusColor(b.status) }}>{statusLabel(b.status)}</div>
              <div style={{ color:T.text3 }}>{open ? "▲" : "▼"}</div>
            </div>

            {open && (
              <div style={{ padding:"0 16px 16px", borderTop:`1px solid ${T.border2}` }}>
                {/* Pipeline steps */}
                <div style={{ display:"flex", gap:8, flexWrap:"wrap", margin:"14px 0" }}>
                  {[
                    { label:"Research", done:!!b.requirements, icon:"🔬" },
                    { label:"Code Gen", done:(b.files||[]).length>0, icon:"⚙️" },
                    { label:"CTO Review", done:!!b.ctoReview, icon:"💻" },
                    { label:"Final QA", done:!!b.finalReview, icon:"🔍" },
                  ].map(s => (
                    <div key={s.label} style={{ display:"flex", alignItems:"center", gap:6, padding:"5px 10px", background:s.done?`${T.green}14`:T.surf2, border:`1px solid ${s.done?T.green+"30":T.border2}`, borderRadius:20, fontSize:"0.7rem", color:s.done?T.green:T.text3 }}>
                      <span>{s.icon}</span><span>{s.label}</span>{s.done && <span>✓</span>}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>

                {b.requirements && (
                  <div style={{ marginBottom:14 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize:"0.68rem", color:T.text3, textTransform:"uppercase", marginBottom:5 }}>Requirements Brief</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize:"0.8rem", color:T.text2, marginBottom:6 }}>{b.requirements.summary}</div>
                    {b.requirements.techStack && <div style={{ fontSize:"0.74rem", color:T.text3 }}>Stack: {b.requirements.techStack}</div>}
                  </div>
                )}

                {(b.files||[]).length > 0 && (
                  <div style={{ marginBottom:14 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize:"0.68rem", color:T.text3, textTransform:"uppercase", marginBottom:6 }}>Generated Files ({b.files.length})</div>
                    <div style={{ background:"#0d1117", border:"1px solid #30363d", borderRadius:10, overflow:"hidden" }}>
                      {b.files.map((f,i) => (
                        <details key={i} style={{ borderBottom: i<b.files.length-1 ? "1px solid #21262d" : "none" }}>
                          <summary style={{ padding:"8px 14px", fontSize:"0.76rem", color:"#e6edf3", cursor:"pointer", fontFamily:"monospace" }}>{f.path}</summary>
                          <pre style={{ margin:0, padding:"12px 16px", overflowX:"auto", fontSize:"0.74rem", lineHeight:1.6, color:"#c9d1d9", background:"#0d1117", maxHeight:280 }}>{f.content}</pre>
                        </details>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {b.ctoReview && (
                  <div style={{ marginBottom:14, background:T.surf2, borderRadius:10, padding:12 }}>
                    <div style={{ display:"flex", justifyContent:"space-between", marginBottom:6 }}>
                      <div style={{ fontSize:"0.76rem", fontWeight:700, color:T.text1 }}>💻 CTO Review</div>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight:800, color: b.ctoReview.score>=80?T.green:b.ctoReview.score>=60?T.yellow:T.red }}>{b.ctoReview.score}/100</div>
                    </div>
                    {(b.ctoReview.issues||[]).map((iss,j)=><div key={j} style={{ fontSize:"0.72rem", color:T.yellow, marginBottom:2 }}>⚠ {iss}</div>)}
                    {(b.ctoReview.suggestions||[]).map((s,j)=><div key={j} style={{ fontSize:"0.72rem", color:T.text2, marginBottom:2 }}>▸ {s}</div>)}
                  </div>
                )}

                {b.finalReview && (
                  <div style={{ marginBottom:14, background:T.surf2, borderRadius:10, padding:12 }}>
                    <div style={{ display:"flex", justifyContent:"space-between", marginBottom:6 }}>
                      <div style={{ fontSize:"0.76rem", fontWeight:700, color:T.text1 }}>🔍 Final QA</div>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight:800, color: b.finalReview.score>=80?T.green:b.finalReview.score>=60?T.yellow:T.red }}>{b.finalReview.score}/100</div>
                    </div>
                    {b.finalReview.feedback && <div style={{ fontSize:"0.74rem", color:T.text2 }}>{b.finalReview.feedback}</div>}
                  </div>
                )}

                {(b.files||[]).length > 0 && (
                  <div style={{ display:"flex", gap:9, flexWrap:"wrap" }}>
                    <button onClick={()=>downloadZip(b.files, b.name)} style={{ padding:"9px 16px", background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${T.accent},${T.accent2})`, border:"none", borderRadius:9, color:"#fff", fontWeight:700, fontSize:"0.78rem", cursor:"pointer" }}>⬇️ Download ZIP</button>
                    <button onClick={()=>{ setGhOpenId(ghOpenId===b.id?null:b.id); setGhMsg(null); }} style={{ padding:"9px 16px", background:T.surf2, border:`1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius:9, color:T.text1, fontWeight:700, fontSize:"0.78rem", cursor:"pointer" }}>🐙 Export to GitHub</button>
                  </div>
                )}

                {ghOpenId === b.id && (
                  <div style={{ marginTop:12, background:T.surf2, border:`1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius:10, padding:14 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize:"0.74rem", color:T.text2, marginBottom:10 }}>Pushes files directly via the GitHub REST API. Needs a personal access token with repo write access. (Works once this app is deployed for real — blocked inside the artifact preview sandbox.)</div>
                    <div style={{ display:"grid", gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr", gap:8, marginBottom:8 }}>
                      <input value={ghOwner} onChange={e=>setGhOwner(e.target.value)} placeholder="GitHub username/org" style={inp} />
                      <input value={ghRepo} onChange={e=>setGhRepo(e.target.value)} placeholder="Repo name" style={inp} />
                      <input value={ghBranch} onChange={e=>setGhBranch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Branch (main)" style={inp} />
                      <input value={ghToken} onChange={e=>setGhToken(e.target.value)} placeholder="Personal access token" type="password" style={inp} />
                    </div>
                    <button disabled={ghPushing} onClick={()=>doGithubPush(b)} style={{ padding:"9px 16px", background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${T.accent},${T.accent2})`, border:"none", borderRadius:9, color:"#fff", fontWeight:700, fontSize:"0.78rem", cursor:ghPushing?"default":"pointer", opacity:ghPushing?0.6:1 }}>{ghPushing?"Pushing...":"Push Files"}</button>
                    {ghMsg && <div style={{ marginTop:8, fontSize:"0.74rem", color: ghMsg.type==="success"?T.green:T.red }}>{ghMsg.text}</div>}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

// ── RESEARCH VIEW ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Dedicated Research Engineer panel: Current Task, Progress, Status,
// Latest Report (full structured breakdown), and Research History.
// ── RESEARCH LAB COMPONENT ────────────────────────────────
const ResearchLabView = ({ S, T, EMPS, dispatch, addActivity, toast, callClaudeJSON, tab, setTab }) => {
  const [labInp, setLabInp] = useState("");
  const [labRunning, setLabRunning] = useState(false);
  const [labStage, setLabStage] = useState("");
  const [selectedAlgo, setSelectedAlgo] = useState(null);

  // Real DNA Simulation Persistent Backend State & Functions
  const [simulations, setSimulations] = useState([]);
  const [simLoading, setSimLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchSimulations = async () => {
    try {
      setSimLoading(true);
      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/simulations`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setSimulations(data);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error fetching simulations:", err);
    } finally {
      setSimLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab === "experiments") {
      fetchSimulations();
    }
  }, [tab, S.proxyUrl]);

  const triggerNewSimulation = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/simulations`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: "PCR Cycle Sim #" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
          type: "PCR Amplification",
          status: "queued",
          progress: 0,
          estimatedTime: "1m 15s"
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast("New simulation triggered and queued!", "success");
        fetchSimulations();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Failed to trigger simulation", "error");
    }
  };

  const cancelSimulation = async (id) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/simulations/${id}`, {
        method: "DELETE"
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast("Simulation removed/cancelled successfully", "info");
        fetchSimulations();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Failed to cancel simulation", "error");
    }
  };

  const resetTelemetryQueue = async () => {
    try {
      for (const sim of simulations) {
        await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/simulations/${sim.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      }
      toast("Telemetry queue reset!", "success");
      fetchSimulations();
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Error resetting queue", "error");
    }
  };

  const runAllQueued = async () => {
    const queuedSims = simulations.filter(s => s.status === "queued");
    if (queuedSims.length === 0) {
      toast("No queued simulations found.", "warning");
      return;
    }

    toast("Processing all queued simulations...", "info");
    for (const sim of queuedSims) {
      try {
        await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/simulations/${sim.id}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ status: "running", progress: 10, estimatedTime: "10s" })
        });
        await fetchSimulations();

        for (let p = 25; p <= 100; p += 25) {
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));
          await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/simulations/${sim.id}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              progress: p,
              status: p === 100 ? "completed" : "running",
              estimatedTime: p === 100 ? "Completed" : `${Math.max(1, (100 - p) / 10)}s`
            })
          });
          await fetchSimulations();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error running simulation:", err);
      }
    }
    toast("All queued simulations processed successfully!", "success");
  };

  // Real DNA Storage Engineering Hooks
  const [dnaStrat, setDnaStrat] = useState("base4");
  const [dnaProcessing, setDnaProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [dnaProgressLogs, setDnaProgressLogs] = useState([]);
  const [dnaProgressPct, setDnaProgressPct] = useState(0);
  const [dnaActiveResult, setDnaActiveResult] = useState(null);
  const [dnaValidationInfo, setDnaValidationInfo] = useState(null);
  const [dnaCompareResult, setDnaCompareResult] = useState(null);
  const [selectedSequence, setSelectedSequence] = useState(null);

  const runRealDNAEncode = async () => {
    if (!labInp.trim()) {
      toast("Please enter a digital payload to encode.", "warning");
      return;
    }
    setDnaProcessing(true);
    setDnaProgressPct(10);
    setDnaProgressLogs(["🧬 [INFO] Initiating Biological Transcription Pipeline..."]);
    setDnaActiveResult(null);
    setDnaValidationInfo(null);
    setDnaCompareResult(null);

    try {
      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(25);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "🔢 [PROCESS] Transforming text payload into digitized bits..."]);

      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(45);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, `⚙️ [ALGORITHM] Compiling bitstream using: ${dnaStrat.toUpperCase()} protocol...`]);

      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/api/dna/encode`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ data: labInp, strategy: dnaStrat })
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json();
        throw new Error(err?.error?.message || "Synthesizer encoding error.");
      }

      const result = await res.json();

      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(70);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "⚖️ [QUALITY] Validating GC Content & checking homopolymer runs..."]);

      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(90);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "📦 [FORMAT] Generating FASTA record & binding SHA-256 integrity hash..."]);

      await sleepMs(300);
      setDnaProgressPct(100);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "✅ [COMPLETE] Synthetic DNA successfully synthesized & committed to DNA storage!"]);

      const dnaItem = {
        id: `dna_${Date.now()}`,
        method: result.strategy,
        size: `${labInp.length * 8} bits`,
        bases: `${result.sequence.length} bp`,
        sequence: result.fasta,
        hash: result.hash,
        original: labInp,
        stats: result.stats,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      dispatch({ type: "ADD_LAB_ITEM", category: "dna", payload: dnaItem });

      const experimentItem = {
        id: `exp_${Date.now()}`,
        hypothesis: `DNA Synthesis Job: ${dnaStrat.toUpperCase()}`,
        accuracy: "100.0% Perfect",
        results: `Encoded payload back to DNA. Hash: SHA256-${result.hash.slice(0, 8)}. Length: ${result.sequence.length} bp. GC Balance: ${result.stats.gcContent}%. Overhead: ${result.stats.overhead}%.`,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      dispatch({ type: "ADD_LAB_ITEM", category: "experiments", payload: experimentItem });

      setDnaActiveResult(result);
      addActivity(`Synthesized DNA sequence using ${dnaStrat.toUpperCase()}`, "🧬");
      toast("DNA synthesis complete!", "success");
    } catch (err) {
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, `❌ [ERROR] Encoding failed: ${err.message}`]);
      toast(err.message, "error");
    } finally {
      setDnaProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const runRealDNADecode = async (fastaContent) => {
    if (!fastaContent) {
      toast("No sequence selected to decode.", "warning");
      return;
    }
    setDnaProcessing(true);
    setDnaProgressPct(15);
    setDnaProgressLogs(["🧪 [INFO] Initializing transcription process from FASTA sequence..."]);
    setDnaActiveResult(null);

    try {
      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(40);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "🧬 [PROCESS] Loading nucleotides into nucleotide alignment map..."]);

      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(70);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "🛡️ [ALGORITHM] Resolving error correction and decoding codons to digital payload..."]);

      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/api/dna/decode`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ sequence: fastaContent })
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json();
        throw new Error(err?.error?.message || "Decolator translation error.");
      }

      const result = await res.json();

      await sleepMs(400);
      setDnaProgressPct(90);
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, `🛡️ [INTEGRITY] Recomputing SHA-256: ${result.hash}...`]);

      await sleepMs(300);
      setDnaProgressPct(100);

      if (result.match) {
        setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "✅ [COMPLETE] Verification success. Original digital payload reconstructed with 100% bit-perfection!"]);
      } else {
        setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, "⚠️ [WARNING] Checksum verification failed! The decoded string could be corrupted."]);
      }

      const experimentItem = {
        id: `exp_${Date.now()}`,
        hypothesis: `DNA Decoding Job: ${result.strategy.toUpperCase()}`,
        accuracy: result.match ? "100.0% (Bit-Perfect Reconstruction)" : "0.0% (Checksum Mismatch)",
        results: `Decoded DNA storage block back to string payload: "${result.decoded.slice(0, 30)}...". SHA-256 match validated: ${result.match ? "PASSED" : "FAILED"}.`,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      dispatch({ type: "ADD_LAB_ITEM", category: "experiments", payload: experimentItem });

      // Update lab input with the decoded payload so they can see/edit it!
      setLabInp(result.decoded);
      addActivity(`Decoded DNA storage block back to string`, "🧬");
      toast("DNA decoded successfully!", "success");
    } catch (err) {
      setDnaProgressLogs(prev => [...prev, `❌ [ERROR] Decoding failed: ${err.message}`]);
      toast(err.message, "error");
    } finally {
      setDnaProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const runRealDNAValidate = async (sequenceString) => {
    if (!sequenceString) {
      toast("No sequence provided for validation.", "warning");
      return;
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/api/dna/validate`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ sequence: sequenceString })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json();
        throw new Error(err?.error?.message || "Validation endpoint failed.");
      }
      const result = await res.json();
      setDnaValidationInfo(result);
      toast("DNA Sequence validation completed", "info");
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, "error");
    }
  };

  const runRealDNACompare = async () => {
    const dataToCompare = labInp.trim() || "APEX Default Test Payload Block";
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE_URL}/api/dna/compare`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ data: dataToCompare })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json();
        throw new Error(err?.error?.message || "Comparison endpoint failed.");
      }
      const result = await res.json();
      setDnaCompareResult(result);
      toast("Strategy Comparison Generated!", "success");
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, "error");
    }
  };

  const runRealDNAExport = (filename, content) => {
    const element = document.createElement("a");
    const file = new Blob([content], { type: "text/plain" });
    element.href = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    element.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(element);
    element.click();
    document.body.removeChild(element);
    toast("FASTA record exported successfully", "success");
  };

  const TABS = [
    { id: "dashboard",   label: "Dashboard",   icon: "📊" },
    { id: "algorithms",  label: "Algo Studio", icon: "📐" },
    { id: "dna",         label: "DNA Lab",     icon: "🧬" },
    { id: "architecture", label: "Pipeline Architect", icon: "🗄️" },
    { id: "staff",       label: "AI Staff",    icon: "🧪" },
    { id: "papers",      label: "Papers",      icon: "📄" },
    { id: "experiments", label: "Experiments", icon: "🔬" },
    { id: "knowledge",   label: "Knowledge",   icon: "🧠" },
    { id: "patent",      label: "Patent Center", icon: "⚖️" },
    { id: "collab",      label: "Collaboration", icon: "🤝" },
  ];

  const subStyle = {
    padding: "8px 12px", borderRadius: 8, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem", fontWeight: 600,
    display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, transition: "all 0.2s",
    background: "transparent", border: "none", color: T.text2
  };

  const activeSubStyle = { ...subStyle, background: `${T.accent}15`, color: T.accent };

  const labLog = (msg, icon="🔬") => { addActivity(msg, icon); setLabStage(msg); };

  const runResearchAction = async (prompt, type, explicitCode = false) => {
    if (!labInp || labRunning) return;
    setLabRunning(true);
    setLabStage("Initializing Research Engine...");

    const onRetry = ({ attempt, elapsed }) => setLabStage(`🔄 Waking up AI... (Attempt ${attempt}, ${elapsed}s)`);

    try {
      const sys = `You are the APEX Research Lab AI. Specialization: ${type}.
      Mandatory Workflow:
      1. Understand the research problem deeply.
      2. Design the optimized algorithm.
      3. Explain the theoretical foundation.
      4. Create mathematical formulas (use plain text representation).
      5. Create an operational workflow.
      6. Generate comprehensive research documentation.

      ${explicitCode ? "ONLY NOW, generate implementation code." : "DO NOT generate source code unless explicitly asked. Focus on the science."}

      Output MUST be valid JSON matching the relevant lab category schema.`;

      const res = await callClaudeJSON(
        [{ role: "user", content: `${prompt}: ${labInp}` }],
        sys, 4000, onRetry
      );

      dispatch({ type: "ADD_LAB_ITEM", category: type, payload: { ...res, id: Date.now(), createdAt: new Date().toISOString() } });
      toast(`Research ${type} completed successfully`, "success");
      setLabInp("");
    } catch (e) {
      toast("Research error: " + e.message, "error");
    }
    setLabRunning(false); setLabStage("");
  };

  // Mock DNA Sequence for visual effect
  const dnaMarquee = useMemo(() => {
    const bases = ["A", "T", "C", "G"];
    return Array.from({length: 100}, () => bases[Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)]).join("");
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ height: "100%", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", background: T.bg }}>
      {/* Sub-Header Navigation */}
      <div style={{ padding: "12px 20px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${T.border}`, display: "flex", gap: 10, overflowX: "auto", background: T.surf, zIndex: 10 }}>
        {TABS.map(t => (
          <button key={t.id} onClick={() => setTab(t.id)} style={tab === t.id ? activeSubStyle : subStyle}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>{t.icon}</span> {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* DNA Visual Marquee */}
      <div style={{ background: `${T.green}08`, borderBottom: `1px solid ${T.green}15`, padding: "4px 20px", overflow: "hidden", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "inline-block", fontSize: "0.6rem", fontFamily: "monospace", color: T.green, letterSpacing: 4, opacity: 0.5 }}>
          {dnaMarquee}{dnaMarquee}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: "auto", padding: 20, position: "relative" }}>
        {/* DASHBOARD */}
        {tab === "dashboard" && (
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(12, 1fr)", gap: 20 }}>
            {/* Active Projects */}
            <div style={{ gridColumn: "span 8", background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 20 }}>
                <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "1.1rem", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
                  <span style={{ color: T.accent }}>⚡</span> Active Research Projects
                </h3>
                <button style={{ padding: "6px 12px", background: T.accent, border: "none", borderRadius: 8, color: "#fff", fontSize: "0.75rem", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>+ New Project</button>
              </div>
              {S.lab.projects.length === 0 ? (
                <div style={{ textAlign: "center", padding: "60px 0", color: T.text3 }}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 40, marginBottom: 15 }}>🧪</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.9rem" }}>No active research pipelines. Initiate an algorithm or DNA study to begin.</div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
                  {S.lab.projects.map(p => (
                    <div key={p.id} style={{ padding: 16, background: T.surf2, borderRadius: 12, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, transition: "transform 0.2s", cursor: "pointer" }}>
                      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: T.text1 }}>{p.title}</div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.green, fontWeight: 700, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>Phase: {p.phase || "Alpha"}</div>
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: "0.78rem", color: T.text2, marginBottom: 15, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{p.summary}</div>
                      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
                        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}><ProgressBar value={p.progress || 20} color={T.accent} height={6} /></div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: "0.7rem", fontWeight: 800, color: T.accent }}>{p.progress || 20}%</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Sidebar Column */}
            <div style={{ gridColumn: "span 4", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
              {/* Timeline Card */}
              <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 20 }}>
                <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", fontSize: "0.9rem", color: T.text1 }}>Research Timeline</h4>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 15 }}>
                  {[
                    { date: "Oct 24", event: "DNA Seq Optimizer v1.2 Release", status: "completed" },
                    { date: "Nov 02", event: "Algorithm Complexity Audit", status: "pending" },
                    { date: "Dec 15", event: "Quantum-Resistant Encoding Lab", status: "planned" }
                  ].map((item, i) => (
                    <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12 }}>
                      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <div style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: "50%", background: item.status === "completed" ? T.green : T.text3 }} />
                        {i < 2 && <div style={{ width: 1, flex: 1, background: T.border2, margin: "4px 0" }} />}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3, fontWeight: 700 }}>{item.date}</div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: item.status === "completed" ? T.text1 : T.text2 }}>{item.event}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Recommendations */}
              <div style={{ background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${T.surf}, ${T.surf2})`, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.accent}30`, padding: 20 }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>💡</span>
                  <h4 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "0.85rem", color: T.accent }}>AI Recommendations</h4>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: "0.78rem", color: T.text2, lineHeight: 1.6, fontStyle: "italic" }}>
                  "Integrating error-correction parity bits into the DNA compression layer could increase storage reliability by 42% for archival data."
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ALGORITHM STUDIO */}
        {tab === "algorithms" && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24, boxShadow: `0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)` }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12, marginBottom: 20 }}>
                <div style={{ width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 10, background: `${T.accent2}20`, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", fontSize: 20 }}>📐</div>
                <div>
                  <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "1.1rem" }}>Algorithm Studio</h3>
                  <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: "0.75rem", color: T.text2 }}>Design, analyze, and protect intellectual breakthrough</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 15, marginBottom: 20 }}>
                <textarea
                  value={labInp} onChange={e=>setLabInp(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Describe your breakthrough idea or complexity problem..."
                  style={{ flex: 1, height: 140, background: T.bg, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 12, padding: 15, color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.88rem", lineHeight: 1.5, resize: "none", outline: "none" }}
                />
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4, 1fr)", gap: 12 }}>
                <button onClick={()=>runResearchAction("Design Algorithm", "algorithms")} disabled={labRunning} style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.accent}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.accent}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.accent, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", transition: "all 0.2s" }}>
                  📐 Designer
                </button>
                <button onClick={()=>runResearchAction("Analyze Complexity", "algorithms")} disabled={labRunning} style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.cyan}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.cyan}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.cyan, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                  📊 Complexity
                </button>
                <button onClick={()=>runResearchAction("Innovation Scoring", "algorithms")} disabled={labRunning} style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.green}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.green}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.green, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                  🌟 Innovation
                </button>
                <button onClick={()=>runResearchAction("Patent Readiness", "algorithms")} disabled={labRunning} style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.accent2}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.accent2}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.accent2, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                  ⚖️ Patent Scan
                </button>
              </div>
              {labRunning && <div style={{ marginTop: 20 }}><ProgressBar label={labStage} value={65} color={T.accent} height={6} /></div>}
            </div>

            {/* Visual Results Grid */}
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fill, minmax(350px, 1fr))", gap: 20 }}>
              {S.lab.algorithms.map(a => (
                <div key={a.id} style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24, transition: "all 0.3s" }}>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", marginBottom: 15 }}>
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: T.text1, fontSize: "1rem" }}>{a.name || "Theoretical Analysis"}</div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3 }}>ID: {a.id} • {new Date(a.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ background: `${T.green}15`, color: T.green, padding: "4px 10px", borderRadius: 20, fontSize: "0.7rem", fontWeight: 800 }}>Ready</div>
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ marginBottom: 15 }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3, textTransform: "uppercase", fontWeight: 800, marginBottom: 8 }}>Foundational Theory</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: "0.8rem", color: T.text2, lineHeight: 1.5, maxHeight: 100, overflow: "hidden", maskImage: "linear-gradient(to bottom, black 60%, transparent)" }}>
                      {a.theory || a.description || "Researching foundation..."}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {a.formulas && (
                    <div style={{ background: T.bg, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, marginBottom: 15, fontFamily: "monospace", fontSize: "0.85rem", color: T.cyan, textAlign: "center", border: `1px solid ${T.cyan}20` }}>
                      {a.formulas}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, borderTop: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, paddingTop: 15 }}>
                    <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: "0.6rem", color: T.text3, marginBottom: 4 }}>Complexity</div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: "0.85rem", fontWeight: 800, color: T.pink }}>O({a.complexity || "n log n"})</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ width: 1, background: T.border2 }} />
                    <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: "center" }}>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: "0.6rem", color: T.text3, marginBottom: 4 }}>Innovation</div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: "0.85rem", fontWeight: 800, color: T.green }}>{a.innovationScore || 85}/100</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* DNA STORAGE LAB */}
        {tab === "dna" && (
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1.2fr 1fr", gap: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
              {/* Encoding Controls Panel */}
              <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 20 }}>
                  <h3 style={{ margin: 0, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
                    <span style={{ color: T.green }}>🧬</span> Biological Encoding Engine
                  </h3>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", color: T.text2, fontWeight: 700 }}>Strategy:</span>
                    <select
                      value={dnaStrat}
                      onChange={e => setDnaStrat(e.target.value)}
                      style={{ background: T.bg, color: T.text1, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 8, padding: "6px 12px", fontSize: "0.75rem", outline: "none", fontWeight: 700 }}
                    >
                      <option value="base4">Base-4 Encoding (Standard)</option>
                      <option value="huffman">Huffman + DNA (Compressed)</option>
                      <option value="reed-solomon">Reed-Solomon DNA (Error-Correcting)</option>
                      <option value="homopolymer-safe">Homopolymer-Safe DNA</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <textarea
                  value={labInp}
                  onChange={e => setLabInp(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Input digital binary, JSON, files or text data for synthetic DNA synthesis..."
                  style={{ width: "100%", height: 110, background: T.bg, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 12, padding: 15, color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem", resize: "none", marginBottom: 15, outline: "none" }}
                />

                <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: 12 }}>
                  <button
                    onClick={runRealDNAEncode}
                    disabled={dnaProcessing}
                    style={{ padding: 12, background: dnaProcessing ? `${T.green}08` : `${T.green}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.green}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.green, fontWeight: 700, cursor: dnaProcessing ? "not-allowed" : "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem" }}
                  >
                    🧬 Encode to DNA
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={runRealDNACompare}
                    disabled={dnaProcessing}
                    style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.cyan}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.cyan}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.cyan, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem" }}
                  >
                    📐 Compare Strategies
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setLabInp("APEX-OS Autonomous DNA Storage Payload. Standard verification hash: SHA-256. Fully functional Reed-Solomon Parity Protection enabled.")}
                    style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.text3}10`, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.text2, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem" }}
                  >
                    📋 Paste Example
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Live Execution Progress Console */}
              {(dnaProcessing || dnaProgressLogs.length > 0) && (
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 12 }}>
                    <h4 style={{ margin: 0, color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                      <span className={dnaProcessing ? "animate-pulse" : ""} style={{ color: T.accent }}>⚙️</span> Synthesizer Progress Console
                    </h4>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", fontFamily: "monospace", color: T.accent, fontWeight: 800 }}>{dnaProgressPct}%</span>
                  </div>
                  {/* Progress Bar */}
                  <div style={{ width: "100%", height: 6, background: `${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 3, overflow: "hidden", marginBottom: 15 }}>
                    <div style={{ width: `${dnaProgressPct}%`, height: "100%", background: T.accent, transition: "width 0.3s ease" }} />
                  </div>
                  {/* Log Console */}
                  <div style={{ background: T.bg, borderRadius: 10, padding: 15, fontFamily: "monospace", fontSize: "0.72rem", color: "#34d399", maxHeight: 160, overflowY: "auto", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}` }}>
                    {dnaProgressLogs.map((log, index) => (
                      <div key={index}>{log}</div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Strategy Comparison Matrix */}
              {dnaCompareResult && (
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                  <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem" }}>DNA Encoding Strategy Comparison</h4>
                  <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
                    <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: "0.75rem", color: T.text2 }}>
                      <thead>
                        <tr style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, textAlign: "left" }}>
                          <th style={{ padding: "8px 4px", color: T.text1 }}>Strategy</th>
                          <th style={{ padding: "8px 4px", color: T.text1 }}>Length (bp)</th>
                          <th style={{ padding: "8px 4px", color: T.text1 }}>Density (bits/nt)</th>
                          <th style={{ padding: "8px 4px", color: T.text1 }}>GC Content</th>
                          <th style={{ padding: "8px 4px", color: T.text1 }}>Overhead</th>
                          <th style={{ padding: "8px 4px", color: T.text1 }}>Homopolymers</th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {Object.entries(dnaCompareResult).map(([strategyName, data]) => (
                          <tr key={strategyName} style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${T.border2}60` }}>
                            <td style={{ padding: "10px 4px", fontWeight: "bold", color: T.text1 }}>{strategyName.toUpperCase()}</td>
                            <td style={{ padding: "10px 4px" }}>{data.success ? `${data.sequenceLength} bp` : "Failed"}</td>
                            <td style={{ padding: "10px 4px" }}>{data.success ? `${data.density.toFixed(2)}` : "-"}</td>
                            <td style={{ padding: "10px 4px" }}>{data.success ? `${data.gcContent}%` : "-"}</td>
                            <td style={{ padding: "10px 4px" }}>{data.success ? `${data.overhead}%` : "-"}</td>
                            <td style={{ padding: "10px 4px", color: data.homopolymerRuns > 0 ? T.pink : T.green }}>
                              {data.success ? (data.homopolymerRuns === 0 ? "Safe (0 runs)" : `Unsafe (${data.homopolymerRuns} runs)`) : "-"}
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Simulation Workspace */}
              <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem" }}>DNA Storage Engine Catalog</h4>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
                  {S.lab.dna.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: "center", padding: "20px 0", color: T.text3, fontSize: "0.78rem" }}>No sequences loaded in the synthesizer. Run "Encode to DNA" first.</div>
                  ) : (
                    S.lab.dna.map(d => {
                      const isSelected = selectedSequence && selectedSequence.id === d.id;
                      return (
                        <div
                          key={d.id}
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedSequence(d);
                            setDnaValidationInfo(null);
                          }}
                          style={{ background: isSelected ? `${T.accent}08` : T.bg, borderRadius: 12, padding: 15, border: `1px solid ${isSelected ? T.accent : T.border2}`, cursor: "pointer", transition: "all 0.2s" }}
                        >
                          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: "0.78rem", fontWeight: 800, color: T.green }}>{d.method.toUpperCase()} Protocol</span>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: "0.7rem", color: T.text3 }}>{d.size} -> {d.bases}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div style={{ fontSize: "0.62rem", fontFamily: "monospace", color: T.text2, overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                            {d.sequence}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* RIGHT COLUMN: Active Result & Diagnostic Analytics */}
            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
              {/* Selected Output / Active Result view */}
              {selectedSequence && (
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                  <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem", display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
                    <span>Active Synthesized Fragment</span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3 }}>SHA-256: {selectedSequence.hash.slice(0, 16)}</span>
                  </h4>

                  {/* FASTA View Box */}
                  <div style={{ background: T.bg, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, fontFamily: "monospace", fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text1, maxHeight: 130, overflowY: "auto", marginBottom: 15, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }}>
                    {selectedSequence.sequence}
                  </div>

                  {/* Fragment Control Panel */}
                  <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10, marginBottom: 20 }}>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedSequence.sequence);
                        toast("Copied FASTA to clipboard", "success");
                      }}
                      style={{ padding: "8px", background: `${T.text3}10`, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 8, color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.7rem", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}
                    >
                      📋 Copy FASTA
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => runRealDNAExport(`apex_dna_${selectedSequence.hash.slice(0, 8)}.fasta`, selectedSequence.sequence)}
                      style={{ padding: "8px", background: `${T.accent}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.accent}30`, borderRadius: 8, color: T.accent, fontSize: "0.7rem", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}
                    >
                      📥 Export File
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => runRealDNADecode(selectedSequence.sequence)}
                      disabled={dnaProcessing}
                      style={{ padding: "8px", background: `${T.yellow}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.yellow}30`, borderRadius: 8, color: T.yellow, fontSize: "0.7rem", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}
                    >
                      🧬 Decode DNA
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => runRealDNAValidate(selectedSequence.sequence)}
                      style={{ padding: "8px", background: `${T.pink}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.pink}30`, borderRadius: 8, color: T.pink, fontSize: "0.7rem", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}
                    >
                      🛡️ Validate DNA
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  {/* Metrics List */}
                  <h5 style={{ margin: "0 0 10px 0", color: T.text2, fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>Physical Properties</h5>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, background: T.bg }}>
                    {[
                      { label: "Sequence Length", value: selectedSequence.bases || `${selectedSequence.sequence.replace(/[^ACGT]/gi, '').length} bp` },
                      { label: "Information Density", value: selectedSequence.stats ? `${selectedSequence.stats.density} bits/bp` : "2.00 bits/bp" },
                      { label: "GC Ratio", value: selectedSequence.stats ? `${selectedSequence.stats.gcContent}%` : "50%" },
                      { label: "Parity Protection Overhead", value: selectedSequence.stats ? `${selectedSequence.stats.overhead}%` : "0%" },
                      { label: "Validation Status", value: selectedSequence.stats && selectedSequence.stats.homopolymerCount === 0 && !selectedSequence.stats.gcImbalance ? "Optimal (No Errors)" : "Requires Audit", color: selectedSequence.stats && selectedSequence.stats.homopolymerCount === 0 && !selectedSequence.stats.gcImbalance ? T.green : T.yellow }
                    ].map((m, i) => (
                      <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
                        <span style={{ color: T.text2 }}>{m.label}</span>
                        <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", color: m.color || T.text1 }}>{m.value}</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Validation Results Panel */}
              {dnaValidationInfo && (
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                  <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
                    <span style={{ color: T.pink }}>🛡️</span> Sequence Audit Diagnostics
                  </h4>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 12 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
                      <span style={{ color: T.text2 }}>Integrity Check:</span>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", color: dnaValidationInfo.isValid ? T.green : T.pink }}>
                        {dnaValidationInfo.isValid ? "PASS (Valid DNA)" : "FAIL (Illegal Char)"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
                      <span style={{ color: T.text2 }}>GC Content Ratio:</span>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", color: dnaValidationInfo.gcImbalance ? T.pink : T.green }}>
                        {dnaValidationInfo.gcContent}% {dnaValidationInfo.gcImbalance ? "(Imbalanced)" : "(Balanced)"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: "0.75rem" }}>
                      <span style={{ color: T.text2 }}>Homopolymer Runs:</span>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", color: dnaValidationInfo.homopolymerRuns.length > 0 ? T.pink : T.green }}>
                        {dnaValidationInfo.homopolymerRuns.length === 0 ? "Excellent (0 runs)" : `${dnaValidationInfo.homopolymerRuns.length} runs detected`}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {dnaValidationInfo.homopolymerRuns.length > 0 && (
                      <div style={{ background: T.bg, padding: 10, borderRadius: 8, fontSize: "0.68rem", color: T.text2, maxHeight: 80, overflowY: "auto" }}>
                        <strong>Detected Runs (Length >= 4):</strong>
                        {dnaValidationInfo.homopolymerRuns.map((r, i) => (
                          <div key={i} style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>
                            Index {r.index}: <span style={{ color: T.pink, fontWeight: "bold" }}>{r.base}</span> x {r.length} bases
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Static Synthesis Estimator Fallback */}
              {!selectedSequence && (
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                  <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 20px 0", color: T.text1, fontSize: "0.85rem" }}>Synthesis Estimator</h4>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 18 }}>
                    {[
                      { label: "Storage Density", value: "215 PB/gram", color: T.green },
                      { label: "Longevity (Cold Storage)", value: "12,000 Years", color: T.cyan },
                      { label: "Synthesis Cost (v4)", value: "$0.02 / GB", color: T.accent },
                      { label: "Read Latency", value: "3.2ms / bp", color: T.yellow },
                      { label: "Error Rate (RS-255)", value: "< 10^-12", color: T.pink }
                    ].map(stat => (
                      <div key={stat.label} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: "0.8rem", color: T.text2 }}>{stat.label}</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: "0.85rem", fontWeight: 800, color: stat.color }}>{stat.value}</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* DNA STORAGE PIPELINE ARCHITECT VIEW */}
        {tab === "architecture" && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
            {/* Horizontal Flow Pipeline Visualizer */}
            <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
              <h3 style={{ margin: "0 0 20px 0", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
                <span style={{ color: T.accent }}>🗄️</span> DNA Storage Pipeline Visualizer
              </h3>

              <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr auto 1fr auto 1fr auto 1fr auto 1fr", alignItems: "center", gap: 10, background: T.bg, padding: 20, borderRadius: 12, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, overflowX: "auto" }}>
                {/* Node 1: Research */}
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", marginBottom: 4 }}>🔬</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", fontWeight: "bold", color: T.text1 }}>1. Research</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3 }}>Dr. Mei Lin</div>
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: "0.65rem", padding: "2px 6px", background: `${T.cyan}20`, color: T.cyan, borderRadius: 4, display: "inline-block" }}>
                    {(S.research || []).length} Papers
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", color: T.text3 }}>➔</div>

                {/* Node 2: Algorithm */}
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", marginBottom: 4 }}>📐</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", fontWeight: "bold", color: T.text1 }}>2. Algorithm</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3 }}>Julian D.</div>
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: "0.65rem", padding: "2px 6px", background: `${T.yellow}20`, color: T.yellow, borderRadius: 4, display: "inline-block" }}>
                    {(S.lab.algorithms || []).length} Algos
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", color: T.text3 }}>➔</div>

                {/* Node 3: DNA Synthesis */}

                  <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", marginBottom: 4 }}>🧬</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", fontWeight: "bold", color: T.text1 }}>3. DNA Lab</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text3 }}>Dr. Elena S.</div>
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: "0.65rem", padding: "2px 6px", background: `${T.green}20`, color: T.green, borderRadius: 4, display: "inline-block" }}>

                    {(S.lab.experiments || []).length} Runs
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", color: T.text3 }}>➔</div>

                {/* Node 5: Recommendation */}
                <div style={{ background: `${T.accent}08`, border: `1px solid ${T.accent}40`, borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "1.2rem", marginBottom: 4 }}>🗄️</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.75rem", fontWeight: "bold", color: T.accent }}>5. Architect</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "0.65rem", color: T.text2 }}>Dr. Marcus V.</div>
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: "0.65rem", padding: "2px 6px", background: `${T.accent}20`, color: T.accent, borderRadius: 4, display: "inline-block", fontWeight: "bold" }}>
                    Active Routing

            {/* Split layout: Actions vs Metrics */}
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1.5fr", gap: 20 }}>
              {/* Left Column: Architect Actions */}
              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
                <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>

                        const reply = await runRealStorageArchitect("Compare Storage Pipelines", "compare");

                      }}
                      style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.cyan}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.cyan}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.cyan, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem" }}

                      style={{ padding: 12, background: `${T.accent}15`, border: `1px solid ${T.accent}30`, borderRadius: 10, color: T.accent, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem" }}

                      🌟 Recommend Best Algorithm

                      ⚙️ Optimize Architecture Parameters
                    </button>

                      S.lab.architecture.map((item, i) => (

                </div>

              {/* Right Column: Comparative Metrics & Visual Output */}
              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
                {dnaCompareResult ? (
                  <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                    <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1 }}>Comparative Parameter Matrix</h4>
                    <div style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", fontFamily: "monospace", fontSize: "0.75rem", background: T.bg, padding: 15, borderRadius: 10, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, color: T.text1, maxHeight: 350, overflowY: "auto" }}>
                      {dnaCompareResult}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : dnaValidationInfo ? (
                  <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                    <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1 }}>Architect Recommendation</h4>
                    <div style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", fontFamily: "monospace", fontSize: "0.75rem", background: T.bg, padding: 15, borderRadius: 10, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, color: T.text1, maxHeight: 350, overflowY: "auto" }}>
                      {dnaValidationInfo}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>
                    <h4 style={{ margin: "0 0 16px 0", color: T.text1 }}>Architect's Multi-Parameter Benchmarks</h4>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>
                      {[
                        { label: "Optimal Synthesis Density", value: "2.3 bits/nt", color: T.green },
                        { label: "Stability Margin Tolerance", value: "98.92% (Perfect)", color: T.cyan },
                        { label: "Max Sequencer Error Limit", value: "< 0.05% (Illumina-Pass)", color: T.yellow },
                        { label: "Parity Correction Overhead", value: "RS-Field @ 50.0%", color: T.pink }
                      ].map((item, i) => (

                      ))}

                        <button
                          onClick={async () => {
                            const reply = await runRealStorageArchitect("Generate Complete System Report", "report");
                            setDnaCompareResult(reply);
                          }}
                          style={{ padding: "10px 16px", background: T.accent, border: "none", borderRadius: 8, color: "#fff", fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "0.78rem" }}
                        >
                          📄 Generate Architecture Report
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* AI STAFF */}
        {tab === "staff" && (
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr))", gap: 20 }}>
            {["scientist", "biologist", "architect", "ai_eng", "data_sci", "patent", "reviewer", "storage_arch"].map(id => {
              const e = EMPS[id];
              return (

                  <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: -20, right: -20, width: 80, height: 80, borderRadius: "50%", background: `${e.color}10`, zIndex: 0 }} />

                  </div>

            })}
          </div>

        {/* RESEARCH PAPERS */}
        {tab === "papers" && (
          <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>

                        <button style={{ flex: 1, padding: "6px", background: T.bg, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 6, color: T.text2, fontSize: "0.65rem" }}>📄 View Abstract</button>
                        <button style={{ flex: 1, padding: "6px", background: T.bg, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, borderRadius: 6, color: T.text2, fontSize: "0.65rem" }}>📥 Export PDF</button>

        {/* COLLABORATION */}
        {tab === "collab" && (
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "2fr 1fr", gap: 20 }}>
            <div style={{ background: T.surf, borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${T.border2}`, padding: 24 }}>

                  { user: "Sarah Kim", action: "pushed complexity audit for DNA-v2", time: "10m ago", icon: "⚙️" },
